# QA run 2026-09-11 — retest R-B1…R-keys + T41–T57 against `main` (0e4dba0, fixes-r6)

Run by QA stream `qa-r3` (worktree `~/dev/inborn-wt/qa-r3`, `main` at 0e4dba0 "Merge branch 'fixes-r6'").
Devices, one at a time: OnePlus 6T (`<6t-serial>`, Android 11, release APK/AAB); emulator `Pixel_4_API_33` (dev build, Metro per tier); iPhone 15 Pro simulator (`12114C34…`, iOS 17.0.1, Debug build-for-testing, driven by `InbornUITests/ScreenshotDriverUITests`).
Evidence under `/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-moshecohen-dev-bibleapps/e1fec2dd-3831-49ec-a78e-d650b5c0d26b/scratchpad/qa-r3/` (`shots/` = Android, `ios-shots/` = simulator, `ios/contact-*.png` = contact sheets). Paths below are relative to that directory.

Tier pretence on emulator/simulator: `EXPO_PUBLIC_TIER=work` / `EXPO_PUBLIC_TIER=pro` baked into the Metro bundle (`apps/mobile/src/license/devFlags.ts`); Free = no flag.

## Totals

| Mac static (release APK/AAB) | 4 (R-B1 static, R-B2, R-B3, R-B5) | 0 | 0 | R-B1 ps16k launch half |

Every R-row of the retest table is PASS on its listed devices except the halves marked "not run" above (R-B1 ps16k launch, R-B10 iOS 26.3). New blockers for the release notes: F1 (vault search leak), F11 (iOS documents lost after an update), F3 (Android SAF name → office gate bypass), F2 (wipe deletes models).

## FAIL list (read first)

| # | Sev | Id | Device | What was seen | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| F1 | High | T48 | emulator (Work) | Chats search returns a chat that lives in a **locked** vault (title + content snippet) and tapping the hit opens the chat without asking the vault code. | `shots/e2-11-search-locked.png`, `shots/e2-12-hit-tapped.png` |
| F2 | Medium | R-B7 | emulator (Free) | "Delete everything" with "Also delete downloaded models" **off** still deletes `files/models/` (vision companion + `vault.json` gone; only the top-level `instant.gguf` survives). `storage/wipe.native.ts` spares only top-level `*.gguf`. | `shots/e-56-after-wipe.png`, `run-as … ls files/models` before/after |
| F3 | Medium | T53 | emulator (Pro) | Picking `budget.xlsx` from the system picker under Pro does **not** show the Work office card: the file is imported as `document:1000000028 · ? · 1.87 KB · This file type is not supported.` The SAF display name is lost (`picked.result.name` is the URI segment), so `sniffPicked` cannot see `.xlsx` and the `officeLocked` gate is bypassed. Same root cause made `policy2.txt` import as `document:1000000027` under Work (T53 Work). | `shots/e3-05-office-card.png`, dump in run log |
| F4 | Medium | B14 | emulator | Reproduced: app theme Dark + system `cmd uimode night no` → chat body and drawer render light while header/settings stay dark, status-bar icons white on a light bar; app restart heals. Mirror case (app Light + system night) is fine. Cold start with lock on shows a white window with white status icons. Release-APK cold-start check not run (time). | `shots/e-41-b14-repro-strip.png`, `e-35-light-drawer.png`, `e-36-light-settings.png`, `e-44-b14-mirror-strip.png`, `e-31-b14-B-coldstart-locked.png` |
| F5 | Medium | T44 / N2 | OnePlus 6T | Read aloud: no TTS engine on the phone → nothing spoken, no user-facing message (see 6T section). | 6T section |
| F6 | Low | T57 | emulator (Work, Pro) | Vault shows a PRO chip on the Sharp cards although the tier owns them (`ModelCard.tsx:105` shows the chip whenever `model.proOnly`). Drawer correctly hides PRO/WORK tags. | `shots/e2-27-vault-work-tags.png` |
| F7 | Low | T49 | emulator (Work) | Audit "Chat moved in" entry carries the chat title (= first user message) — the log is promised to hold no message content. | `shots/e2-25-audit-7.png` |
| F8 | Low | T53 | emulator | Citation labels "§N" are chunk ordinals, not the document's own section numbers (passage "POLICY.HTML · §3" opens "## §1 Scope"). | `shots/e2-50-passage-s3.png` |
| F9 | Low | a11y | emulator | Unlabelled switches: report-include, thinking-switch, redact/doc-select toggles; Translator persona chip clipped in Chat settings (bounds 1014–1025); unlabelled node at [965,2119,1020,2174] in the drawer. | `a11y` dumps in run log |
| F10 | Low | dev | emulator | Dev bundle logs "Can't perform a React state update on a component that hasn't mounted yet" (ExpoRoot.js:135) on every cold start with the lock on; LogBox toast then swallows the first tap at the bottom of the screen. | `elogs` in run log |
| F11 | High (iOS) | T53 / documents | simulator | Documents are stored with **absolute** `file://` URIs (`documents/files.native.ts` `copyIntoLibrary` returns `dest.uri`, kept as `doc.uri`). iOS moves the app data container to a new UUID on every reinstall/update (each xcodebuild run here, and every App Store or TestFlight update): the copy is still on disk under `Documents/documents/` but Resume/index fails with "The file is damaged or not what its name says." (`i03-indexed.png`), while the same bytes re-imported after the move index fine (`j03-reindexed.png`). Every imported document is lost after an app update on iPhone. The vault stores file names, so models survive. Same risk for `files/images` photo paths in messages (not verified). | `i03-indexed.png`, `j03-reindexed.png`, container paths `3FA3E8BA…` → `1A5594D6…` in the run log |

## Environment notes for the next run

- Emulator: LogBox toast at y≈1933–2064 swallows the first bottom tap after every cold start with the lock on (F10); FLAG_SECURE blanks `screencap` while a passcode is set (turn "Hide in app switcher" off for screenshot evidence); the emulator crashed once under host load ~100 (other streams building) and was restarted with `-no-snapshot-load`, app data intact.
- Simulator: the app container remembers `RCT_jsLocation`; with Simulator.app "Connect Hardware Keyboard" on, XCUITest `typeText` into the passcode sheet produced "Wrong passcode." while the same code typed with the software keyboard unlocks — disconnect the hardware keyboard before keyboard checks.
- `EXPO_PUBLIC_MODELS_BASE_URL=http://localhost:8790/v1` + `scripts/serve-models.mjs` serve the embed/vision companions to the simulator; Android uses `10.0.2.2`.

### Observations (not failures)

- O1 (item 2): with Fast as the vault default on a 4 GB phone the app loads the 1.2 GB model at boot and only then switches (36 s here); no boot-time check against the RAM floor.
- O2 (item 1): a chat aged 1 d 23 h 59 m under "7 days" reads "Deletes in 6 days" (ceil of 5.0007); it flips to "5 days" a minute later.
- O3 (item 5): Fix grammar left "me and him" (Instant 0.8B quality); Replace still returns the result correctly.
- O4 (items 4, 3): `quick-target`, `quick-result` and the vault details footer are not visible to uiautomator by id (Markdown/Pressable children); driven by text instead.
- Disk: the emulator's 6 GB data partition needed room for Fast (`requiredFreeBytes` = bytes + 2 GiB reserve → "Free up 872 MB"); freed by removing a stale `/data/local/tmp/instant.gguf`, `pm trim-caches`, and the embed + vision companions via the `dev-vault.txt` hook (`remove <id>`). Fast is left installed and as the default on this AVD.

Process audit (end of pass 2): emulator `emulator-5620` killed (`emu kill`), Metro pids 21068 / 67321 / 95124 / 1524 and serve-models 21021 killed by pid, simulator shut down; `pgrep -fl "qemu-system|expo start|serve-models|GradleDaemon|xcodebuild"` shows only other streams' processes (Metro port 8117, a Pixel_6 emulator).

### New findings

| ID | Sev | Item | What | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F17 | Medium (Work) | F1 / vaults | "Unvault" on the chat's folder-manage sheet removes a chat from a **locked** vault without asking for the vault code. Repro (Work): vault "Client A" with one chat, chip → Lock (drawer shows CLIENT A · LOCKED) → open the chat's folder sheet (`folder-pick` / manage) → `vault-remove-<id>` → the chat reappears in the drawer as a plain chat ("Reply with one word: zebra deposit"), no `vault-code` sheet, vault still LOCKED. Search and open are guarded (F1) but the move-out path is not, so a locked vault's content can be read by unvaulting it. | `p3-14-unvault-while-locked.png`, `p3-w2.log` ("probe: Unvault from the manage sheet while LOCKED") |
| F18 | High (intermittent) | F15 / storage | Native crash in `libexpo-sqlite.so` when the chat handle is closed underneath the repository: `tombstone_27` 13.9 22:15:13, pid 8782 thread `DefaultDispatch`, `signal 6 (SIGABRT)`, abort message `Scudo ERROR: invalid chunk state when deallocating address 0x2000071e8c954d0`, frames `exsqlite3_free+112` ← `exsqlite3_finalize+156` (`exsqlite3_close` on the same stack). It fired on the first launch after `adb install -r` vc2 while the dev hook (`EXPO_PUBLIC_DEAD_DB_AFTER_MS=20000`) closed the handle; 1 crash in 8 hook firings today (runs 3–8 clean). The hook only reproduces what expo-sqlite does when a handle is closed while a statement is being finalized, so the JS reopen (F15) is not enough on its own: the close must wait for in-flight statements (or the native close must be serialized behind them). | `f18-tombstone.txt` (full tombstone, 6,345 lines), `p3-f12.log` |

### Observations (not failures)

- O1 (F13): in the Chats drawer the device banner is drawn over the search field (`chats-search` at y 244–368 sits under "Ran out of memory · Switched to Instant" at y 244–294): the banner inset is applied to the chat screen but not to the drawer (`p3-01-boot-floor-banner.png` vs `p3-02-drawer-two-chats.png`); tapping the field's lower half still focuses it.
- O2 (F8/documents): on the Documents screen "Install · 262 MB" for the embedder did nothing under the needs-space state (no sheet, no message); the vault card for the same model showed "Free up N MB". After freeing space (`dev-vault.txt` `remove fast`, `install embed-nomic`) it installed.
- O3 (documents): right after the embedder install the first Ask returned the raw `no-embedder` state until the app was relaunched.
- O4 (Settings): once, `inborn://settings` opened after a return from the launcher left a full-screen `lock-screen` node on top and Settings ignored every tap (theme rows, switches); after `am force-stop` + relaunch it responded and the same deep link worked. Not reproduced in three later attempts (`p3-cover.log`).
- O5 (harness, not the app): `adb shell input text` with two `r` keystrokes within ~200 ms while the composer was **not** focused (a LogBox toast had swallowed the tap) triggers React Native's dev "double-tap R" reload — `Running "main"` again, passcode sheet back, no chat created (runs 3–6, `f15-run5-full.log` 22:42:28). Each `console.warn` (the F15 reopen line) re-raises the toast over the sheet's bottom button. Fixed in the harness by clearing the toast right before the tap, checking `focused="true"` on the `EditText`, and using r-free prompts; the F15/F12 results above are from the clean runs 7–8.
- O6 (simulator): the shared simulator has the Hebrew keyboard active (`AppleKeyboards` `he_IL`, languages `en-US, he-US`); the English question typed into the Ask field renders right-aligned with the "?" at the left (`q05-ask-answer.png`, already so in pass 1 `j07-ask-keyboard.png`). iOS takes the paragraph direction from the keyboard language of an empty field; a Hebrew-keyboard user typing English would see the same. Not verified on a device.
- O7 (harness): an "Apple ID Verification" system alert (the simulator's signed-in account) appeared once over the app (`m01`–`m03`) and stole keyboard focus (`type` failed: "Neither element nor any descendant has keyboard focus"); it did not return in the four later runs. The simulator's account was not touched.
- O8 (F11 method): `xcodebuild test-without-building` reinstalls the app on every `launch`, so each driver run moves the data container (`CBC11B6C…` → `D618D4D7…` → `345D82CC…` → `32D971C8…` → `DFAB3F58…` → `3CC7FFE9…` today); the F11 record survived all of them.

### Per row

**T06 Download cancelled.** Emulator: Vault › Fast (1.28 GB over the throttled HTTPS path) → "Delivering FAST · 41 %" → Cancel: `files/models/*.gguf.part` gone within 1 s, `df /data` used dropped 3,530,160 → 3,293,988 KB, `vault.json` `downloads: {}`, card back to "Install"; a second Cancel after a fresh Install at 47 % was equally clean (`12-fast-delivering.png`, `13-crop.png`, `14-fast-cancelled.png`). **T07 Storage full.** Emulator, three halves. (a) Before: 1.0 GB free (`fallocate` in `files/fill.bin`), tap Install on Fast: the confirm sheet still opens, then the card reads "Free up 2.1 GB" + "Try again" and no request reaches the model server (`16-t07-needs-space.png`; `serve-models.log` silent). Passes the intent of §10.1 #5; wording differs from the checklist's "needs X, you have Y". (b) During: disk filled while Fast was delivering → card shows the raw native text "Could not install: Call to function 'FileSystemDownloadTask.start' has been rejected. → Caused by: Unable to download a file: write failed: ENOSPC (No space left on device)", a 264 MB `.part` is kept, and the vault header still said "144 MB free" while `df` showed 32 KB (`17-t07-enospc.png`, `18-t07-enospc-2.png`). After freeing space and a relaunch the card reads "Paused · 252 MB of 1.2 GB"; Resume issued `GET … Range: bytes=264294400-1280835839` (206, byte-exact resume, `26-t07-resume-after-free.png`, `27-t07-resumed.png`). (c) During DB write: chat with 32 KB free. The first send was answered; later sends rendered one assistant row with the raw text "Call to function 'NativeDatabase.execAsync' has been rejected. → Caused by: cannot rollback - no transaction is active" (`20-…`, `21-t07-send-full-disk-15s.png`, `22-t07-three-more.png`), and the dev LogBox showed "Uncaught Error … FileSystemFile.write … ENOSPC" every 5 s from `writePrefsRaw` (`src/services/prefsStore.native.ts:25`, called by the exit-meter tick in `AppServices.tsx:276`, `22-crop.png`). After freeing space + relaunch: prefs recovered from `prefs.bak.json` (`[prefs] prefs.json unreadable`), no onboarding, the chat row survived but held only the first user message: three exchanges were lost with no notice (`23-t07-after-relaunch.png`, `24-t07-drawer-after.png`, `25-t07-chat-reopened.png`). The `state.storageFull` banner never appeared (unreferenced in code). Simulator half: WAIVER (the container reports the host's free space).

**T13 Call / lock.** Emulator, Instant streaming a 3000-word story: `adb emu gsm call 5551234` → heads-up "Incoming call" over the chat, streaming continued (app stayed `topResumedActivity`); `gsm accept` → call ACTIVE, streaming slowed to ~1 tok/s and continued; `gsm cancel` → back to full speed (`30-t13-streaming.png`, `31/32/33-t13-call-*.png`, `t13-call-sheet.png`). `KEYCODE_SLEEP` for 25 s → Metro `[inborn] answer paused: background grace expired`, `[device] paused … button=continue`; on wake the strip reads "Paused while Inborn was in the background. The partial answer is kept." + CONTINUE and the row "The system stopped generation · Continue"; CONTINUE resumed (Stop back, `[device] normal`), the DB row kept the partial (`34-t13-after-lock.png`, `35-t13-paused-banner.png`, `36-t13-continued.png`). Simulator: Home for 30 s (`XCUIDevice.press(.home)`) → same strip and row (`ios/contact-b.png`, `ios-shots/d01-paused.png`, Metro `answer paused: background grace expired`); the row's Continue resumed (Stop visible at 8 s, continuation prompt 1,540 tokens in Metro) but the "Paused…" strip stayed up until its own CONTINUE was tapped (`d02-row-continue-8s.png`, `d03-banner-continue-8s.png`, F17); one earlier XCTest tap on the row (run B, `b03-continued.png`) did not register, treated as a driver miss. 6T: WAIVER. The SAF picker cannot be steered to Downloads by keys, so the 6T imports were abandoned; fixtures removed from its `/sdcard/Download`, launcher in front.
- O10 (simulator, dev only): the `AUTOINDEX` hook re-imports on every Documents mount, so a relaunch produced duplicate `corrupt.pdf` / `empty.pdf` rows ("10 documents", `f01-docs-top.png`).
- Dev-build proof screen: the airplane verdict text contradicted the meter because of Metro traffic; not a release path.

Driver change: `apps/mobile/ios-tests/ScreenshotDriverUITests.swift` gained `home`, `activate`, `typein:<id>:<text>` (taps until the field reports keyboard focus), `key:return|shift-return` and `value:<id>` steps (needed for T13 / T28 on the simulator); the generated copy under `ios/` is not committed.

### New observations (not failures)

- O9 (documents, Medium-low): the mirror of O3 is missing. Removing the embedder from the vault in the same session (`remove embed-nomic` hook, `files/models` left with `vault.json` only) leaves Documents with **no** install card: `p4-27a-o2-documents-after-removal-same-session.png` shows the plain empty library, while a relaunch shows the card (`p4-27-o2-documents-needs-space.png`). `watchEmbedder` only re-checks on "ready", so the library keeps `kind: "ready"` with a path that no longer exists until the next boot.
- O10 (vault, once): the first hook install after a boot with `/data` short (fill removed just before the install) ended `[vault] embed-nomic failed · verified in 381 ms` although the 274,290,560-byte file was on disk, and Documents then showed "Could not install: …/files/models/nomic-embed-text-v1.5.f16.gguf: open failed: ENOENT" with Install (`p4-29-o3-documents-after-vault-install.png`, `p4-30-vault-embed-state.png` "Try again"). The retry from the vault card installed normally, and two later installs (UI and hook) succeeded; not reproduced.
- O11 (documents): after the space is freed the "Free up 216 MB" line stays on the Documents and vault cards until the next Install tap (`p4-38-o2-documents-after-freeing.png`); a re-check when the screen regains focus would clear it.
- Harness: `uiautomator dump` returns nothing while the vault card animates a download (known), so install completion was read from `files/models/vault.json` `installs` and the `[vault] … ready` log line.

### New finding

| # | Sev | Item | What | Fixed? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F20 | High | R4-F14 | **Every HTTPS model install fails on 617e23a** (regression from de55843; pass 4a on 3ea3274 installed the same embedder, O3). `apps/mobile/src/vault/httpsDelivery.ts:109-111`: inside the new `catch`, `throw e;` comes before `this.ctx.saveDownload(model.id, null); this.finish(shard, part);`, so the `.part` → final rename never runs on success; verification then opens the missing final file → "Could not install: … open failed: ENOENT". Reproduced 3 of 3 (03:24:10, 03:27:56 on a free disk with a fresh `.part`, 03:32:49 after the ENOSPC resume). Second effect: Try again with the full-size `.part` gets `resumePlan` "done", which `taskFor` treats as a fresh download (deletes the part, `GET 200` from byte 0, 03:24:53 run). The 121 mobile unit tests did not catch it: no test drives a completed `deliverPart` through the rename. Fix: move the two lines back into the `try` after the `if (!result)` block, add a test that asserts the final file exists after a completed download. | No |

### Observations (not failures)

- O12 (T26, iOS, Medium): the row Pressable now carries `accessibilityRole="text"` + `accessibilityLabel` (`AssistantMessage.tsx:38-42`); on iOS that merges the row into one accessibility element, so the inline Continue (`testID="continue"`), Regenerate, the reasoning toggle, LEDGER and citation chips inside it are no longer separate elements: `idb ui describe-all` listed the "Paused while…" strip and its CONTINUE but no `continue` element while the row's Continue was on screen (`i02b-return-no-alert.png`). VoiceOver users cannot reach those controls; Android keeps the children (the T26 tree shows them). The row needs `accessible={false}` with the label on the text, or `accessibilityActions` for continue/regenerate.
- O13 (R4-F14, same as O11): after the space was freed the card kept "Free up 2.3 GB" for 12 s until Try again was tapped (`p4b-f14b.log`).
- O14 (R4-F13/F14): `[storage] disk full · 0 bytes free` is logged while `df -k /data` shows 16 MB free; `usableDiskBytes` floors at 0 (fine for the strip, misleading in the log).
- Harness: `fallocate -l $((expr))` inside `adb shell "…"` is evaluated by the device shell and fails ("Invalid argument"); compute the byte count on the Mac and pass a literal. On the simulator `idb ui tap` points are not the screenshot pixels ÷ 3 on this device (÷ 2.34 matched the composer and Send); read frames from `describe-all`. The shared simulator raised its "Sign in to iCloud" alert again (Cancel tapped, nothing entered).

### New finding

| # | Sev | Item | What | Fixed? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F21 | Medium | `formatBytes` | **Every size that rounds to a whole number ending in 0 is printed ten times too small.** `packages/core/src/proof/exitMeter.ts:33` ends with `v.toFixed(digits).replace(/\.?0+$/, "")`; the regex is meant to drop trailing zeros *after a decimal point*, but when `digits === 0` (any value at 100 or more of its unit) it eats a significant zero: `formatBytes(524288000)` → **"5 MB"** for 500 MB, `formatBytes(104857600)` → **"1 MB"** for 100 MB, `formatBytes(125829120)` → **"12 MB"** for 120 MB. Seen on device three times out of three: the Documents embedder card read "Free up 81 MB" at 1,572,859,904 B usable against a 2,421,774,208 B requirement (real shortfall 848,914,304 B = "810 MB"), and "Free up 31 MB" at 2,097,147,904 B usable (shortfall 324,626,304 B = "310 MB"); at 1,258,287,104 B usable it printed the correct "1.08 GB", because values that keep a decimal are unaffected — which is why passes 3–4b never hit it. The **vault** card is right in the same state ("Free up 1.1 GB") because it formats with `formatModelBytes` (`packages/core/src/catalog/resume.ts:64`), which is correct; only the surfaces on `formatBytes` are wrong: the Documents storage line, document size and details, the embedder card, the onboarding model line, the delivery banners, the Proof / airplane exit meter, Settings › Storage and the web download screen. Roughly one size in ten is understated by 10× there. The unit loop is also mixed (`while (v >= 1000)` but `v /= 1024`), which is why 1,500 B prints as "1.46 KB". All 7 `formatBytes` cases in `packages/core/test/exit-meter.test.ts` use values that keep a decimal, so none of them catches it. None of the seven catalog sizes is affected (508 MB / 1.19 GB / 2.55 GB / 2.32 GB / 262 MB / 141 MB / 195 MB all print correctly), so the damage is on **computed** numbers: shortfalls, document and library sizes, downloaded/remaining bytes and the exit meter. Repro: `node -e` on the function, or the Documents card at any fill leaving a shortfall of N00 MB. | No |

### Observations (not failures)

- O15 (harness): a leftover `files/dev-vault.txt` from an earlier stream is read **and deleted** by the VaultScreen dev hook (`VaultScreen.tsx:140`, 1.5 s poll, only while the vault screen is mounted) the first time the vault is opened, and its command runs with no log line. In the first O9 attempt that silently removed the 262 MB embedder between opening Documents and opening the vault, which looked like an app bug. Clear `files/dev-vault.txt` at the start of a run when `EXPO_PUBLIC_AUTOINSTALL=file` is set.
- O16 (harness): two traps cost ~40 minutes. (a) `confirm-download` sits at y 2104–2225, exactly where the LogBox toast renders; a tap sent before the confirm sheet finishes animating silently misses, and the run reads as "Try again does nothing" (twice: no HEAD, no GET, no state change for 122 s and 184 s). Tap it again once `ui dump` still shows the sheet. (b) the pass-3 `lb()` helper *opens* the LogBox to press Dismiss, which attaches DevTools ("Launching DevTools…" in the Metro log); with the debugger attached the download progress replayed at about 1 % per 2.5 s and a verify that takes 1,144 ms took 12,727 ms. Dismiss the toast with its own ✕ instead (`lbx()` in `lib.sh`).
- O17 (dev build only): `adb shell` is not root by default on this AVD, so `sendevent` for the hardware-keyboard row needs `adb root` first; `adb root` restarts adbd and drops every `adb reverse`, so re-add the Metro and model-server ports right after.

(pid 95919) killed by pid, no simulator booted during this pass; `pgrep -fl "qemu-system|expo start|serve-models|GradleDaemon|xcodebuild"`
showed only the other stream's `Pixel_6_API_33` emulator, its Metro on 8137 and its `serve-models`, nothing of mine.

### New findings

| # | Sev | Item | What | Fixed? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F22 | Medium | Web tier model download | **A browser download of Instant intermittently stores a file that fails its hash**, and the door shows the red "Download failed: stored file does not match" with a "Download 533 MB" button. Seen **4 times in 16 first attempts** (25 %) on the same machine and the same 533 MB source file, whose sha256 matches the catalog (`shasum -a 256 .models/Qwen3.5-0.8B-Q4_K_M.gguf` = `bd258782…c517`, the manifest value). It is **not** the cancel/Range-resume path: two failures came from `web:smoke` runs that cancel and resume (runs 1 and 6, resume offsets 12 MB both times, while a third run resumed from the same 12 MB and passed), and two more from a straight download with no cancel at all (`scripts/web-advice.mjs`). **A second tap on Download succeeds**: in `logs/web-retry-a.log` attempt 1 failed and attempt 2 landed the model and reached the composer, so the user is not stuck — but a third of first-time browser visitors see a failure message on a 533 MB download. All four failures fell in the earlier, more heavily loaded half of the run (a gradle build or several browser runs alongside the emulator); the last four runs, with less work in parallel, all landed on the first attempt. That is a hint, not a conclusion, but with the source file's hash verified on disk it points at a write/verify race in the OPFS store rather than at the network. Repro: `SMOKE_OUT_DIR=/tmp/x pnpm web:smoke` in a loop, or `node scripts/web-advice.mjs`; evidence `logs/web-smoke.log`, `logs/web-smoke-6.log`, `logs/web-advice.log`, `logs/web-retry-a.log`, `web/web-advice-fail.png`. | No |
| F23 | Medium | §7.8 on the web tier | **The browser tier has no honest mediation at all.** A Hebrew prompt on Instant in headless Chromium answers with pure gibberish (digits and Chinese, `web/web-advice-fail.png`) and shows **neither** the "INSTANT is weak in Hebrew" caption **nor** the advice card. Both surfaces are switched off by an explicit platform guard, neither of them commented: the card at `apps/mobile/src/screens/Chat.tsx:715` (`if (Platform.OS === "web" \|\| !lastUserText) return null;` — `adviseModel` is never even called on web) and the caption at `apps/mobile/src/screens/Chat.tsx:738` (`if (Platform.OS === "web" \|\| !adviceLanguage) return null;`). The phone user, who *can* switch, is warned; the browser user, who cannot, is not. The caption needs no alternative model to be useful and is exactly the §6.3 "mediate honestly" promise. Repro: `node scripts/web-advice.mjs`. | No |

### Observations (not failures)

- O18: the Details sheet heading reads **"GOOD LANGUAGES"** but the list under it now carries every tier, Basic included ("… Hebrew (Basic)"). The heading predates the fit map; "LANGUAGES" would match what it shows (`shots/p5-3-07-details-fast-langs.png`).
- O19: `formatBytes` still mixes its thresholds — `while (v >= 1000)` but `v /= 1024` — so a value between 1000 and 1023 of a unit jumps a unit early: 1000 B prints "0.98 KB" and 1,048,575 B prints "1 MB". Pass 4c noted the same loop. It is cosmetic and separate from the F21 class, which is closed.
- O20 (harness): `uiautomator dump` reports **invalid bounds** (bottom smaller than top, e.g. `[81,404][666,83]`) for React Native buttons sitting near a scroll edge, so a tap helper that trusts the first match silently taps the wrong place. `scripts/tap.py` validates `y2 > y1`, nudges the list with a slow 700 ms drag and re-dumps; a fast 200 ms swipe flings the target off screen instead.
- O21 (harness): the O16a trap has a second home — the LogBox toast also covers the **new-chat sheet's Start button**, and its ✕ carries no `content-desc`, so a "Dismiss" lookup finds nothing and the run silently continues in the previous chat (which then eats the `dev-prompt.txt` message, because every mounted Chat polls that file). Tap the ✕ by position (x ≈ 996, y ≈ 2233 at 1080×2400) before driving that sheet, and check `composer-input` is present before queuing a prompt.
- O22: the once-per-chat rule is stricter than it first looks — a reason already shown in a chat does not come back in that chat for the rest of the app run even after other messages, and the card always reflects the **last** user message. Two intermediate "no card" readings during the 5-minute mix were this rule plus a stale last message, not a defect: after a force-stop the same chat showed the code card again. Worth a line in the spec, and worth knowing before filing it as a bug. Evidence `shots/p5-o22-advice-card-present-after-nav.png`: the card **is** on screen after the navigation round trip ("SHARP (PHI) is better at code than FAST." · `Install SHARP (PHI) · 2.3 GB` · PRO · Not now). The file was first named `p5-f-advice-lost-after-nav.png` while that hypothesis was still open; it was renamed once the screenshot disproved it, so nothing in F22–F26 corresponds to it.
- O23 (harness): this app has no `expo-dev-client`, so `inborn://expo-development-client/?url=…` does nothing and the debug build falls back to the bundle in assets, which does not exist → a red box "Unable to load script". It reads the packager host from the default SharedPreferences, and it resolves `10.0.2.2`, not `localhost`, so `adb reverse tcp:8081` cannot redirect it. To run Metro on a private port, stop the app and write `<string name="debug_http_host">10.0.2.2:<port></string>` into `shared_prefs/com.inbornapp.mobile_preferences.xml` with `run-as`.
- O24 (harness): `adb push` of the 533 MB Instant GGUF into the app's data directory needs `adb root`; `adb root` and `adb unroot` each restart adbd and drop every `adb reverse`, so re-add the Metro and model-server ports afterwards (the pass-4c O17 note applies to `root` as well as to `sendevent`).
- O25 (harness, iOS): `idb ui describe-all` reports frames in the **scroll content** coordinate space, so a row below the fold comes back with a y beyond the 852-pt screen (the Fast Install button read y = 1108) and a tap there silently misses. `scripts/scrolltap.py` swipes the list until the target's centre sits between 120 and 760 pt and only then taps.
- O26 (harness, iOS): the first `idb ui tap` issued right after a swipe is swallowed by the scroll momentum; the same tap repeated a second later opens the sheet. Two taps with a screenshot between them is the reliable pattern.
- O27 (harness, iOS): while a React Native `Modal` is presented — the download confirm sheet, both "Best for" pickers, ModelDetails — `idb ui describe-all` returns **only the application root**, so those sheets have to be driven by coordinates read off a screenshot (the simulator is 393×852 pt for a 1179×2556 px image, divide by 3).
- O28 (iOS): the simulator has no speech service at all; the composer mic answers "Voice error: Connection to speech process was invalidated or interrupted." Everything else on the voice surface (the mic button, the error sheet) renders correctly, so this is a simulator limit, not app behaviour.
- O29 (harness, iOS): Metro on a private port did not work for this build — the app started with "No script URL provided" and `unsanitizedScriptURLString = (null)`, and writing `RCT_jsLocation` into the app's preferences plist did not change it. Metro on the default 8081 works; check the port is free rather than moving it.

Process audit (end of pass 5): emulator `emulator-5642` killed (`adb emu kill`, qemu pid 71982 gone), Metro 8143 and `serve-models` 8801 killed by pid,
every headless Chromium from the web runs exited with its script. `pgrep -fl "qemu-system|expo start|serve-models|GradleDaemon|chrome-headless|web-smoke|web-advice"`
returns nothing of this stream's, `adb devices` shows only the OnePlus 6T (another stream's phone, never touched by this run), and
`xcrun simctl list devices booted` is empty after the iOS half (see below).

Process audit (end of the iOS half, 15:47): simulator `12114C34-288A-4AAB-B50A-693DC6775C60` shut down (`xcrun simctl shutdown`;
`xcrun simctl list devices booted` now lists nothing at all). iOS Metro on 8081 (pid 36230) and `serve-models` on 8801 (pid 94859) killed by pid;
both ports free (`lsof -nP -iTCP:8081/8801 -sTCP:LISTEN` empty). What is still running on the machine belongs to **other streams**, not to this one:
`qemu-system-aarch64-headless -avd Pixel_6_API_33_ll -port 5640`, `expo start --port 8147`, five Playwright chrome-headless-shell processes started
after this pass's browser runs had exited, two Gradle daemons, and six long-lived `idb_companion` sockets (the one for this simulator's UDID has
been up since 11.9, so it predates this run). `adb devices` shows `<6t-serial>` (the OnePlus 6T, another stream's soak phone) and `emulator-5640`
(another stream's emulator); this pass's own `emulator-5642` is gone. Neither the 6T nor the physical iPhone was touched in this pass.

Send is out of the ring only while the draft is empty, because it is disabled then. The soak drove the 6T through the accessibility-service test APK on vc6; that, not the chips, is where the difference has to be. |
| F27, TAB focus trap on the empty chat — **retest 21.9.2026, overturns the row above** | **REPRODUCED on the OnePlus 6T, now FIXED** | The emulator verdict was wrong. On vc8 on the 6T (Android 11, 384 dp) a fresh empty chat gives `composer-input → mic → Dismiss → summarize → translate → draft → translate → draft → …` for ever (`docs/qa/soak-run-4-2026-09-21.md`, section F27). Root cause is React Native 0.86's `ReactScrollView.focusSearch`: under `enableCustomFocusSearchOnClippedElementsAndroid` it discards any next focus that is not one of its own descendants and substitutes one from inside the scroll view, so TAB can never leave a list. Fixed on branch `fixes-r15` by `apps/mobile/plugins/withScrollFocusEscape.js` (the flag off) plus a declared `nextFocusForward` chain chips → composer in `apps/mobile/src/screens/Chat.tsx`. Eighteen TABs on the 6T now give two identical nine-stop rings that reach `composer-input` each time (README "Fixes round 15", shots in `docs/qa/fixes-r15/`). |
| F29, warm ACTION_SEND document import | NOT REPRODUCED, harness artifact | `am start --grant-read-uri-permission` from the shell creates no grant at all (`dumpsys activity permissions` is empty), and the shell cannot itself read `content://com.android.externalstorage.documents/…Download/…` without ACTION_OPEN_DOCUMENT, so the app's `openInputStream` fails and nothing is imported — cold and warm alike. With a URI the app can read, both paths import: cold `shots/f29-01-cold-share.png`, warm (`am` answering "delivered to currently running top-most instance") `shots/f29-03-warm-share-works.png`. Separate finding for the next round, not F29 and not warm-specific: sharing a file whose earlier import failed returns the failed twin from `documents/library.ts`, and the chat flashes "could not read" and attaches nothing. It bites cold and warm equally, and on this emulator every import fails because the 262 MB index model is not installed. |

### New findings

Numbered from **F30**: the round-12 table above already uses "F29" for the warm `ACTION_SEND` row.

| # | Sev | Item | What | Fixed? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F30 | Low | Settings still prints `NULL` for the model | F25 was fixed on the drawer only. `apps/mobile/src/screens/Settings/Settings.tsx:152` renders the CHAT → Model row as `<Row label={t("settings.chat.model")} value={engine.model.id.toUpperCase()} … />`, with no empty state and without `meterLabel`, so a user who has not installed a model yet sees **`Model    NULL`** on the Settings screen — the same raw id `chats.tsx` stopped showing. By the same line an imported model will show its raw file id here while the drawer shows the friendly name. Repro: fresh install → "Stay on Instant only" → Settings → CHAT section. Evidence `shots/p6-f-settings-model-null.png`. Fix is one call: `meterLabel(engine.model.id, t("onboarding.model.none"))`. | No |
| F31 | Low | The details sheet lists Chinese three times | `apps/mobile/src/screens/vault/ModelDetails.tsx:44` maps **every** key of `fit.languages`, including the script variants, while the cartridge (`FitMap.tsx:22`) deliberately hides a script variant that agrees with its plain language. Fast and Sharp both carry `zh`, `zh-Hans` and `zh-Hant` at the same tier, so their Languages row reads "English (Native), **Chinese (Native), Chinese (Simplified) (Native), Chinese (Traditional) (Native)**, Spanish (Good) …" — three Chinese entries in a row, two of them with a tier in a second pair of brackets after a name that already ends in brackets. In zh-Hant the brackets also mix widths: "中文（繁體） (母語級)". Same on Android and iOS and in every locale. Repro: vault → Fast → Details. Evidence `shots/p6-f-details-chinese-three-times.png`, `shots/p6-3-ko-04-details.png`, `shots/p6-3-zh-05-details.png`, `ios-shots/i06-ko-details.png`. The one-line fix is the filter `FitMap` already has. | No |
| F32 | Low | `ledger.msPerToken` is untranslated in zh-Hant only | `packages/i18n/locales/zh-Hant.json` keeps `"MS / TOKEN"` while `ja.json` has `"MS / トークン"` and `ko.json` `"MS / 토큰"`. It is the only key where zh-Hant leaves an English word that its two CJK siblings translate, so it reads as an oversight rather than a brand term. Evidence: the locale sweep in this pass (`ko` and `zh-Hant` each 1013 keys, 28 identical-to-English values; this key is inside zh-Hant's 28 and outside ko's and ja's). | No |

### Observations (not failures)

- O30 (harness): `uiautomator dump` reports **invalid bounds** (bottom above top, e.g. `[81,404][666,214]`) for the vault's `install-*` and `use-*` buttons whenever the card sits near a scroll edge — the O20 trap, but far more frequent on a long vault. `scripts/tap.py` refuses those and nudges, and the nudge it ships (a 700 ms 250 px swipe) usually moves the target off screen again. A small settling swipe (`input swipe 540 1200 540 1500 700`) and a re-dump produced sane bounds every time.
- O31 (harness): `am start -n com.inbornapp.mobile/com.inbornapp.sharetarget.ProcessTextActivity -a android.intent.action.PROCESS_TEXT --es …` fails with "Activity class … does not exist" although `pm dump` lists it. Adding **`-t text/plain`** makes the same command start it. The activity is fine; the intent was not resolvable without a type.
- O32 (harness): `adb logcat -T 1 | grep -m1 …` never returns on an idle app — `grep` exits but `adb logcat` only notices at the next log line, so the pipeline hangs forever. Poll `adb logcat -d | grep -c` after `adb logcat -c` instead; that is also what makes a 3-second window measurable.
- O33 (device): loading **Sharp (Phi) (2.49 GB) on a 6 GB guest** works, but the device guard then does its job — `Ran out of memory · Switched to Instant` with a `SWITCH BACK` action, and `banner-memory` appears in the drawer. Nothing crashed (one pid, 0 tombstones), and the caption/card evidence for row 2d was captured before the switch. A 4 GB AVD cannot hold Phi at all.
- O34: the **model chip in the drawer footer is a label, not a link** — it carries no `content-desc` and `clickable="false"`, and the MODEL row inside the Chat-settings sheet is not tappable either. The only route to the vault from inside the app is Settings → CHAT → Model. Worth knowing before scripting, and possibly worth a second entry point for users.
- O35 (harness, iOS): `idb ui swipe` moves the settings list about **845 pt** per gesture regardless of the distance asked for when the endpoints are far apart, so a "scroll until the row is on screen" loop overshoots in both directions and never converges. Endpoints 200 pt apart (`200 600 → 200 400`) move roughly what they say; `scripts/ioslib.sh:ifind` uses that and lands in two or three steps.
- O36: one Continue tap on the paused banner did **not** resume — no new `[stats]`, and the Chats drawer opened instead — but the same tap at the same coordinates worked on a clean repeat, and again on a third run, so it is filed as unreproduced rather than as a finding. If it ever shows up again the thing to capture is whether `emitShortcut("continue")` reached a shortcut handler.
- O37: the round-12 fix for **F26** cannot be exercised on a development build that has `EXPO_PUBLIC_MODELS_BASE_URL` set, because the https delivery is then offered and the card shows "Install · 508 MB from 127.0.0.1" instead of the Play-unavailable line. The row has to be run with that variable unset, which is what this pass did.
